(() => {
  "use strict";
  const $ = (s, c = document) => c.querySelector(s),
    $$ = (s, c = document) => [...c.querySelectorAll(s)];
  const esc = (s) =>
    String(s).replace(
      /[&<>"']/g,
      (c) =>
        ({
          "&": "&amp;",
          "<": "&lt;",
          ">": "&gt;",
          '"': "&quot;",
          "'": "&#39;",
        })[c],
    );
  const reduced = matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce)").matches;
  const rid = (p) =>
    "#PESO-" + p + "2026-" + (1000 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 9000));
  let user = null,
    timers = [],
    wiz = 1,
    wdata = {};

  function toast(m, t = "ok") {
    const e = document.createElement("div");
    e.className = "toast " + t;
    e.innerHTML =
      "<span>" +
      esc(m) +
      '</span><button type="button" aria-label="Dismiss notification">Ã—</button>';
    $("#toasts").append(e);
    let h;
    const k = () => {
        e.classList.add("out");
        setTimeout(() => e.remove(), 260);
      },
      a = () => {
        h = setTimeout(k, 5000);
      };
    a();
    e.onmouseenter = () => clearTimeout(h);
    e.onmouseleave = a;
    $("button", e).onclick = k;
  }

  function theme(t) {
    document.documentElement.dataset.theme = t;
    try {
      localStorage.setItem("peso-theme", t);
    } catch (e) {}
    $$(".themeBtn").forEach((b) => {
      const d = t === "dark",
        l = $(".lbl", b);
      if (l) {
        l.textContent = d ? " Light mode" : " Dark mode";
        b.setAttribute("aria-label", d ? "Light mode" : "Dark mode");
      } else b.textContent = d ? "Light mode" : "Dark mode";
    });
  }
  let sv;
  try {
    sv = localStorage.getItem("peso-theme");
  } catch (e) {}
  theme(
    sv ||
      (matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme:dark)").matches ? "dark" : "light"),
  );
  document.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
    if (e.target.closest(".themeBtn"))
      theme(
        document.documentElement.dataset.theme === "dark" ? "light" : "dark",
      );
  });

  const F = (o) => {
    const rq = o.r === false ? "" : " required",
      d = (o.h ? o.id + "-h " : "") + o.id + "-err";
    let c;
    if (o.cb)
      return `<div class="field full"><div class="ck"><input type="checkbox" id="${o.id}" name="${o.id}"${rq} aria-describedby="${d}"><label for="${o.id}">${o.l}</label></div><span class="error" id="${o.id}-err"></span></div>`;
    if (o.opts)
      c = `<select id="${o.id}" name="${o.id}"${rq} aria-describedby="${d}"><option value="">Select an option</option>${o.opts.map((x) => `<option>${x}</option>`).join("")}</select>`;
    else if (o.ta)
      c = `<textarea id="${o.id}" name="${o.id}"${rq} ${o.a || ""} aria-describedby="${d}"></textarea>`;
    else {
      c = `<input id="${o.id}" name="${o.id}" type="${o.t || "text"}"${rq} ${o.a || ""} aria-describedby="${d}">`;
      if (o.t === "password")
        c = `<div class="pw">${c}<button type="button" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Show password">Show</button></div>`;
    }
    return `<div class="field${o.full ? " full" : ""}"><label for="${o.id}">${o.l}${o.r === false ? "" : ' <span class="req" aria-hidden="true">*</span>'}</label>${c}${o.h ? `<span class="hint" id="${o.id}-h">${o.h}</span>` : ""}<span class="error" id="${o.id}-err"></span></div>`;
  };
  document.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
    const b = e.target.closest(".pw button");
    if (!b) return;
    const i = $("input", b.parentNode),
      s = i.type === "password";
    i.type = s ? "text" : "password";
    b.textContent = s ? "Hide" : "Show";
    b.setAttribute("aria-pressed", s);
    b.setAttribute("aria-label", s ? "Hide password" : "Show password");
  });
  const MOB =
      'pattern="^(\\+63|0)9\\d{9}$" inputmode="tel" autocomplete="tel" placeholder="09123456789"',
    PW = 'minlength="8" autocomplete="new-password"';
  const EDU = [
    "High school graduate",
    "Vocational diploma",

    "College graduate",

  const SUB = '<div class="acts"><button class="btn btn-p" type="submit">';

  function msg(el) {
    const l = (el.labels[0] ? el.labels[0].textContent : "this field")
        .replace("*", "")
        .trim()
        .toLowerCase(),
      v = el.validity;
    if (v.valueMissing)
      return el.type === "file"
        ? "Attach your resume to continue."
        : el.type === "checkbox"
          ? "Accept the data privacy consent to continue."
          : el.tagName === "SELECT"
            ? "Select " + l + "."
            : "Enter " + l + ".";
    if (v.typeMismatch)
      return "Enter a valid email address, such as name@example.com.";
    if (v.patternMismatch)
      return el.id === "vpay"
        ? "Enter pay in pesos, such as â‚±18,000â€“22,000/mo."
        : "Use a valid mobile number, such as 09123456789.";
    if (v.tooShort) return "Enter at least " + el.minLength + " characters.";
    if (v.rangeUnderflow)
      return el.type === "date"
        ? "Choose today or a later date."
        : "Enter " + el.min + " or more.";
    if (v.rangeOverflow) return "Enter " + el.max + " or less.";
    return "Check this entry.";
  }
  function vf(el) {
    let m = "";
    if (!el.checkValidity()) m = msg(el);
    else if (el.type === "file" && el.files[0]) {
      const f = el.files[0];
      if (!/\.(pdf|docx?)$/i.test(f.name)) m = "Upload a PDF or Word file.";
      else if (f.size > 5242880)
        m = "File is larger than 5 MB. Upload a smaller file.";
    }
    el.closest(".field").classList.toggle("bad", !!m);
    el.setAttribute("aria-invalid", !!m);
    const er = $("#" + el.id + "-err");
    if (er) er.textContent = m;
    return !m;
  }
  function vForm(f) {
    const bad = $$("input,select,textarea", f).filter((x) => !vf(x)),
      al = $(".alert", f.closest(".wpanel,.fp,.dlg,.card") || document);
    if (al) {
      al.textContent = bad.length
        ? "Please correct " +
          bad.length +
          (bad.length > 1 ? " fields" : " field") +
          " before continuing."
        : "";
      al.classList.toggle("show", !!bad.length);
    }
    if (bad.length) {
      bad[0].focus();
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  }
  function live(f) {
    $$("input,select,textarea", f).forEach((x) => {
      x.addEventListener("blur", () => {
        if (x.value || x.closest(".field").classList.contains("bad")) vf(x);
      });
      ["input", "change"].forEach((v) =>
        x.addEventListener(v, () => {
          if (x.closest(".field").classList.contains("bad")) vf(x);
        }),
      );
    });
  }

  const tg = $("#navToggle"),
    lk = $("#navLinks"),
    sc = $("#scrim");
  function drawer(o) {
    lk.classList.toggle("open", o);
    sc.classList.toggle("on", o);
    tg.setAttribute("aria-expanded", o);
    tg.setAttribute("aria-label", o ? "Close menu" : "Open menu");
    if (o) $("a,button", lk).focus();
  }
  tg.onclick = () => drawer(!lk.classList.contains("open"));
  sc.onclick = () => {
    drawer(false);
    tg.focus();
  };
  lk.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
    if (e.target.closest("a,button")) drawer(false);
  });
  document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
    if (!lk.classList.contains("open")) return;
    if (e.key === "Escape") {
      drawer(false);
      tg.focus();
    }
    if (e.key === "Tab") {
      const f = [tg, ...$$("a,button", lk)],
        i = f.indexOf(document.activeElement);
      if (e.shiftKey && i <= 0) {
        e.preventDefault();
        f[f.length - 1].focus();
      } else if (!e.shiftKey && i === f.length - 1) {
        e.preventDefault();
        f[0].focus();
      }
    }
  });
  matchMedia("(min-width:901px)").addEventListener("change", () =>
    drawer(false),
  );
  $("#noticeX").onclick = () => $("#notice").remove();

  let rays = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < 24; i++) {
    const big = i % 3 === 0;
    rays += `<line x1="0" y1="-84" x2="0" y2="${big ? -190 : -140}" transform="rotate(${i * 15})" stroke-width="${big ? 3 : 1.5}"/>`;
  }
  $("#sun").insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend", rays);
  $$("[data-count]").forEach((el) => {
    const to = +el.dataset.count,
      t0 = performance.now(),
      f = (n) => {
        const p = Math.min((n - t0) / 1500, 1);
        el.textContent = Math.round(
          to * (1 - Math.pow(1 - p, 3)),
        ).toLocaleString("en-PH");
        if (p < 1) requestAnimationFrame(f);
      };
    reduced
      ? (el.textContent = to.toLocaleString("en-PH"))
      : requestAnimationFrame(f);
  });
  let vac = 1248;
  setInterval(() => {
    if (document.hidden || $("#landing").hidden) return;
    vac += 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);
    $("#cVac").textContent = vac.toLocaleString("en-PH");
    $("#upd").textContent =
      "Sample figures. Last tick: " +
      new Date().toLocaleTimeString("en-PH", {
        hour: "2-digit",
        minute: "2-digit",
        second: "2-digit",
      });
  }, 7000);
  const SK = [

  function setJoin(role, focus) {
    const j = role === "jobseeker";
    $("#tabJ").setAttribute("aria-selected", j);
    $("#tabE").setAttribute("aria-selected", !j);
    $("#tabJ").tabIndex = j ? 0 : -1;
    $("#tabE").tabIndex = j ? -1 : 0;
    $("#pJ").hidden = !j;
    $("#pE").hidden = j;
    if (focus) $("#tab" + (j ? "J" : "E")).focus();
  }
  $$("[data-role]").forEach((b) => {
    b.onclick = () => setJoin(b.dataset.role);
    b.onkeydown = (e) => {
      if (/Arrow(Left|Right)/.test(e.key))
        setJoin(
          b.dataset.role === "jobseeker" ? "employer" : "jobseeker",
          true,
        );
    };

  $$("[data-join]").forEach((a) =>

  $("#wbody").innerHTML = WP.map(

  function go(n) {
    const d = n >= wiz ? "next" : "prev";
    wiz = n;
    $$(".wpanel").forEach((p) => {
      const on = +p.dataset.step === n;
      p.hidden = !on;
      p.classList.remove("next", "prev");
      if (on) {
        void p.offsetWidth;
        if (!reduced) p.classList.add(d);
      }
    });
    $("#wbar").style.width = (n / 3) * 100 + "%";
    $("#wprog").setAttribute("aria-valuenow", n);
    $("#wprog").setAttribute("aria-valuetext", "Step " + n + " of 3");
    $$("#wsteps li").forEach((li, i) => {
      li.className = i + 1 === n ? "on" : i + 1 < n ? "done" : "";
      $("i", li).textContent = i + 1 < n ? "âœ“" : i + 1;
      i + 1 === n
        ? li.setAttribute("aria-current", "step")
        : li.removeAttribute("aria-current");
    });
    const f = $(".wpanel:not([hidden]) input,.wpanel:not([hidden]) select");
    if (f) f.focus({ preventScroll: true });
  }
  $$("#wbody form").forEach((f) => {
    live(f);
    f.addEventListener("submit", (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (!vForm(f)) return;
      $$("input:not([type=file]):not([type=password]),select", f).forEach(
        (x) => (wdata[x.name] = x.value.trim()),
      );
      const n = +f.dataset.s;
      if (n < 3) {
        toast("Step " + n + " saved.");
        go(n + 1);
        return;
      }
      const id = rid("");
      wdata.id = id;
      $("#wizard").hidden = true;
      $("#confirm").hidden = false;
      $("#cId").textContent = id;
      $("#cName").textContent = wdata.fullName;
      $("#cMail").textContent = wdata.email;

        block: "center",
        behavior: reduced ? "auto" : "smooth",
      });
      $("#cGo").focus({ preventScroll: true });
    });
  });
  $$("[data-back]").forEach((b) => (b.onclick = () => go(+b.dataset.back)));

  const SKEY = "peso-session",
    V = { landing: $("#landing"), js: $("#jsView"), em: $("#emView") };
  let db,
    tag = "All",
    saved = new Set(),
    applied = new Set(),
    open = new Set(),
    NOTES = [],
    cur = null,
    cvName = "",
    pend = "",
    ivI = null,
    last = null;
  const adlg = $("#applyDlg"),
    af = $("#af");
  const stopTimers = () => {
    timers.forEach(clearTimeout);
    timers = [];
  };
  const saveSession = () => {
    try {
      user
        ? localStorage.setItem(SKEY, JSON.stringify(user))
        : localStorage.removeItem(SKEY);
    } catch (e) {}
  };
  const today = () =>
    new Date(Date.now() - new Date().getTimezoneOffset() * 6e4)
      .toISOString()
      .slice(0, 10);
  const sub = (t) =>
    `<div class="acts"><button class="btn btn-gold" type="submit">${t}</button></div>`;
  function companyFrom(m) {
    const d = (m.split("@")[1] || "company").split(".")[0];
    return d.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + d.slice(1);
  }
  function clearForm(f) {
    f.reset();
    $$(".field.bad", f).forEach((x) => x.classList.remove("bad"));
    $$(".error", f).forEach((x) => (x.textContent = ""));
    $$("[aria-invalid]", f).forEach((x) => x.removeAttribute("aria-invalid"));
    const a = $(".alert", f.closest(".dlg,.card") || f);
    if (a) a.classList.remove("show");
  }

  /* ---- view controller ---- */
  function show(v) {
    Object.keys(V).forEach((k) => {
      V[k].hidden = k !== v;
    });
    $("#appbar").hidden = v === "landing";
    $("#skip").href =
      v === "landing" ? "#main" : v === "js" ? "#jsMain" : "#emMain";
    V[v].classList.remove("vin");
    void V[v].offsetWidth;
    V[v].classList.add("vin");
  }
  function enter(u, quiet) {
    stopTimers();
    user = u;
    saveSession();
    if (dlg.open) dlg.close();
    const js = u.role === "jobseeker",
      nm = js ? u.name : u.co || u.name;
    $("#rolePill").textContent = js ? "Jobseeker" : "Employer";
    $("#appSub").textContent = js
      ? "Jobseeker dashboard"
      : "Employer command center";
    $("#meName").textContent = nm;
    $("#av").textContent = nm.charAt(0).toUpperCase();
    $("#bell").hidden = !js;
    pop(false);
    show(js ? "js" : "em");
    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
    document.title = (js ? "Dashboard" : "Command center") + " | PESO";
    js ? initJs() : initEm();
    if (!quiet)
      toast("Welcome, " + u.name.split(" ")[0] + ". You are now logged in.");
    $(js ? "#jsMain" : "#emMain").focus({ preventScroll: true });
  }
  function logout() {
    stopTimers();
    pop(false);
    [adlg, $("#dJ"), $("#dI")].forEach((d) => d.open && d.close());
    user = null;
    saveSession();
    show("landing");
    document.title = "Public Employment Service Office";
    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
    toast("You have been logged out.");
  }
  $("#logout").onclick = logout;
  $("#appHome").onclick = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  };

  /* ---- notifications ---- */
  function notes() {
    const n = NOTES.filter((x) => x[2]).length;
    $("#nl").innerHTML = NOTES.map(
      (x) =>
        `<li class="${x[2] ? "new" : ""}">${esc(x[0])}<time>${x[1]}</time></li>`,
    ).join("");
    $("#badge").hidden = !n;
    $("#badge").textContent = n;
    $("#bell").setAttribute(
      "aria-label",
      "Notifications, " + (n ? n + " unread" : "none unread"),
    );
  }
  function pop(o) {
    $("#pop").hidden = !o;
    $("#bell").setAttribute("aria-expanded", o);
  }
  $("#bell").onclick = () => pop($("#pop").hidden);
  $("#readAll").onclick = () => {
    NOTES.forEach((x) => (x[2] = 0));
    notes();
    toast("All notifications marked as read.");
  };
  document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
    if (e.key === "Escape" && !$("#pop").hidden) {
      pop(false);
      $("#bell").focus();
    }
  });
  document.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
    if (!$("#pop").hidden && !e.target.closest("#pop,#bell")) pop(false);
  });

  /* ---- jobseeker dashboard ---- */
  function initJs() {
    const u = user;
    saved = new Set();
    applied = new Set();
    open = new Set();
    tag = "All";
    cvName = u.resume || "";
    NOTES = [
      ["Welcome to PESO Online. Browse job offers below.", "Just now", 1],
      ["New job fair: Mega Job Fair on Nov 14, 2026.", "Today", 1],
    ];
    $("#jsHi").textContent = "Welcome, " + u.name.split(" ")[0];
    $("#jsId").textContent = u.id || "";
    $("#q").value = "";
    $("#jsLoc").innerHTML =
      '<option value="">All locations</option>' +
      [...new Set(J.map((j) => j[2]))]
        .map((l) => `<option>${l}</option>`)
        .join("");
    $("#chips").innerHTML = TAGS.map(
      (t) =>
        `<button type="button" aria-pressed="${t === "All"}" data-t="${t}">${t}</button>`,
    ).join("");
    track();
    stats();
    notes();
    render();
    [
      [1, 6000, "Your application is now under review."],
      [2, 15000, "Good news: an interview has been scheduled."],
    ].forEach(([s, t, m]) => {
      if ((u.stage || 0) < s) timers.push(setTimeout(() => step(s, m), t));
    });
  }
  function step(s, m) {
    if (!user || user.role !== "jobseeker") return;
    user.stage = s;
    saveSession();
    track();
    stats();
    NOTES.unshift([m, "Just now", 1]);
    notes();
    toast(m);
  }
  const track = () => {
    $("#tkBox").innerHTML = timeline(user.stage || 0);
  };
  const stats = () => {
    $("#sSaved").textContent = saved.size;
    $("#sApps").textContent = 1 + applied.size;
    $("#sInt").textContent = (user.stage || 0) >= 2 ? 1 : 0;
  };
  function render() {
    const q = $("#q").value.toLowerCase().trim(),
      l = $("#jsLoc").value;
    const L = J.map((j, i) => ({ j, i })).filter(
      ({ j }) =>
        (tag === "All" || j[4].includes(tag)) &&
        (!l || j[2] === l) &&
        (!q || (j[0] + j[1] + j[5] + j[6].join(" ")).toLowerCase().includes(q)),
    );
    $("#cnt").textContent = L.length
      ? L.length + " job offer" + (L.length > 1 ? "s" : "") + " found"
      : "No job offers match your filters. Clear the search or choose All.";
    $("#grid").innerHTML = L.map(({ j, i }) => {
      const a = applied.has(i),
        o = open.has(i);
      return `<article class="card jc" aria-labelledby="t${i}"><div class="tags">${j[4].map((t) => `<span class="tag${t === "Remote" ? " r" : ""}">${t}</span>`).join("")}</div><h3 id="t${i}">${j[0]}</h3><p class="cname">${j[1]}</p><div class="meta"><span>ğŸ“ ${j[2]}</span></div><p class="pay">${j[3]}</p>${o ? `<div class="det" id="d${i}"><p>${j[5]}</p><ul>${j[6].map((r) => `<li>${r}</li>`).join("")}</ul></div>` : ""}<div class="row"><button class="btn btn-p" type="button" data-a="${i}"${a ? " disabled" : ""}>${a ? "Applied" : "Apply Now"}</button><button class="btn btn-line" type="button" data-v="${i}" aria-expanded="${o}"${o ? ` aria-controls="d${i}"` : ""}>${o ? "Hide details" : "View Details"}</button><button class="btn btn-line" type="button" data-s="${i}" aria-pressed="${saved.has(i)}" aria-label="${saved.has(i) ? "Unsave" : "Save"} ${esc(j[0])}">${saved.has(i) ? "â˜…" : "â˜†"}</button></div></article>`;
    }).join("");
  }
  $("#chips").onclick = (e) => {
    const b = e.target.closest("button");
    if (!b) return;
    tag = b.dataset.t;
    $$("#chips button").forEach((x) => x.setAttribute("aria-pressed", x === b));
    render();
  };
  $("#q").oninput = render;
  $("#jsLoc").onchange = render;
  $("#grid").onclick = (e) => {
    const a = e.target.closest("[data-a]"),
      v = e.target.closest("[data-v]"),
      s = e.target.closest("[data-s]");
    if (v) {
      const i = +v.dataset.v;
      open.has(i) ? open.delete(i) : open.add(i);
      render();
      $('[data-v="' + i + '"]').focus();
    }
    if (s) {
      const i = +s.dataset.s;
      saved.has(i) ? saved.delete(i) : saved.add(i);
      stats();
      render();
      $('[data-s="' + i + '"]').focus();
      toast(saved.has(i) ? "Job saved." : "Job removed from saved.");
    }
    if (a) {
      cur = +a.dataset.a;
      af.reset();
      pend = "";
      $("#rf").setCustomValidity("");
      $("#rf").required = !cvName;
      $("#rn").textContent = cvName
        ? cvName + " (on file)"
        : "No resume on file yet";
      $("#re").textContent = $("#ce").textContent = "";
      $("#rs").classList.remove("bad");
      $("#dJob").textContent = J[cur][0];
      $("#dMeta").textContent = J[cur][1] + ", " + J[cur][2] + ", " + J[cur][3];
      adlg.showModal();
    }
  };
  const closeApply = () => {
    adlg.close();
    const b = $('[data-a="' + cur + '"]');
    b && b.focus();
  };
  $("#dx").onclick = $("#dc").onclick = closeApply;
  adlg.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
    if (e.target === adlg) closeApply();
  });
  /* resume check via Constraint Validation: required + custom validity for type/size */
  function cvCheck() {
    const rf = $("#rf"),
      f = rf.files[0];
    let m = "";
    if (f) {
      if (!/\.(pdf|docx?)$/i.test(f.name)) m = "Upload a PDF or Word file.";
      else if (f.size > 5242880)
        m = "File is larger than 5 MB. Upload a smaller file.";
    }
    rf.setCustomValidity(m);
    return m;
  }
  $("#rf").onchange = () => {
    const m = cvCheck(),
      f = $("#rf").files[0];
    $("#re").textContent = m;
    $("#rs").classList.toggle("bad", !!m);
    if (f && !m) {
      pend = f.name;
      $("#rn").textContent = f.name + " (new)";
    }
  };
  af.addEventListener("submit", (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const rf = $("#rf"),
      ok = $("#ok");
    cvCheck();
    const rm = rf.checkValidity()
        ? ""
        : rf.validity.valueMissing
          ? "Attach your resume to continue."
          : rf.validationMessage,
      cm = ok.checkValidity() ? "" : "Confirm the statement above to submit.";
    $("#re").textContent = rm;
    $("#rs").classList.toggle("bad", !!rm);
    $("#ce").textContent = cm;
    ok.setAttribute("aria-invalid", !!cm);
    if (rm || cm) {
      (rm ? rf : ok).focus();
      return;
    }
    if (pend) cvName = pend;
    applied.add(cur);
    adlg.close();
    stats();
    render();
    toast("Application sent to " + J[cur][1] + " for " + J[cur][0] + ".");
    NOTES.unshift([
      "You applied to " + J[cur][0] + " at " + J[cur][1] + ".",
      "Just now",
      1,
    ]);
    notes();
    const b = $('[data-v="' + cur + '"]');
    b && b.focus();
  });

  ["#fJ", "#fI", "#fH"].forEach((s) => live($(s)));
  function initEm() {
    db = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DB0));
    $("#emHi").textContent = user.co || user.name;
    $("#hD").min = $("#iD").min = today();
    renderAll();
    mtab(user.tab === "assist" ? "h" : "p");
  }
  function mtab(v, f) {
    $$("#mt button").forEach((b) => {
      const on = b.dataset.v === v;
      b.setAttribute("aria-selected", on);
      b.tabIndex = on ? 0 : -1;
      if (on && f) b.focus();
    });
    $$("#emView .view").forEach((p) => (p.hidden = p.id !== "v-" + v));
  }
  $("#mt").onclick = (e) => {
    const b = e.target.closest("button");
    if (b) mtab(b.dataset.v);
  };
  $("#mt").onkeydown = (e) => {
    if (!/Arrow(Left|Right)/.test(e.key)) return;
    const a = $$("#mt button"),
      i = a.findIndex((b) => b.getAttribute("aria-selected") === "true");
    mtab(
      a[(i + (e.key === "ArrowRight" ? 1 : a.length - 1)) % a.length].dataset.v,
      true,
    );
  };
  const cls = (s) => s.split(" ")[0],
    lbl = (s) => (s === "Pending" ? "Pending approval" : s);
  function renderAll() {
    const Jb = db.jobs,
      A = db.apps;
    $("#s1").textContent = Jb.filter((j) => j[3] === "Active").length;
    $("#s2").textContent = Jb.filter((j) => j[3] === "Pending").length;
    $("#s3").textContent = A.length;
    $("#s4").textContent = A.filter((a) => a[4].startsWith("Interview")).length;
    $("#jb").innerHTML = Jb.map(
      (j, i) =>
        `<tr><td><b>${esc(j[0])}</b>${j[5] ? `<small>${esc(j[5])}</small>` : ""}</td><td>${esc(j[1])}</td><td>${j[4] ? esc(j[4]) : "Not stated"}</td><td>${j[2]}</td><td>${A.filter((a) => a[3] === i).length}</td><td><span class="b ${j[3]}">${lbl(j[3])}</span></td><td>${j[3] === "Pending" ? '<span class="hint">Awaiting PESO</span>' : `<button class="btn btn-line sm" type="button" data-tog="${i}">${j[3] === "Active" ? "Close posting" : "Reopen"}</button>`}</td></tr>`,
    ).join("");
    const sel = $("#fp").value;
    $("#fp").innerHTML =
      '<option value="">All postings</option>' +
      Jb.map((j, i) => `<option value="${i}">${esc(j[0])}</option>`).join("");
    $("#fp").value = sel;
    renderApps();
    $("#rb").innerHTML = db.reqs.length
      ? db.reqs
          .map(
            (r) =>
              `<tr><td><b>${r[0]}</b></td><td>${esc(r[1])}</td><td>${r[2]}</td><td><span class="b Pending">Received</span></td></tr>`,
          )
          .join("")
      : '<tr><td colspan="4" class="empty">No requests yet. Send one using the form above.</td></tr>';
  }
  function renderApps() {
    const p = $("#fp").value,
      s = $("#fs").value,
      L = db.apps
        .map((a, i) => ({ a, i }))
        .filter(({ a }) => (p === "" || a[3] == +p) && (!s || cls(a[4]) === s));
    $("#ac").textContent =
      L.length + " applicant" + (L.length === 1 ? "" : "s") + " shown";
    $("#ab").innerHTML = L.length
      ? L.map(({ a, i }) => {
          const f = cls(a[4]),
            done = f === "Rejected";
          return `<tr><td><b>${esc(a[0])}</b><small>${esc(a[1])}, ${esc(a[2])}</small></td><td>${esc(db.jobs[a[3]][0])}</td><td><button class="btn btn-line sm" type="button" data-cv="${i}" aria-label="Download resume of ${esc(a[0])}">Download</button></td><td><span class="b ${f}">${esc(a[4])}</span></td><td><div class="ra"><button class="btn btn-line sm" type="button" data-st="${i}:Shortlisted"${f !== "New" ? " disabled" : ""}>Shortlist</button><button class="btn btn-p sm" type="button" data-iv="${i}"${done ? " disabled" : ""}>Schedule interview</button><button class="btn btn-line sm" type="button" data-st="${i}:Rejected"${done ? " disabled" : ""}>Reject</button></div></td></tr>`;
        }).join("")
      : '<tr><td colspan="5" class="empty">No applicants match these filters.</td></tr>';
  }
  $("#fp").onchange = $("#fs").onchange = renderApps;
  $("#emView").addEventListener("click", (e) => {
    const t = e.target.closest(
      "[data-tog],[data-st],[data-iv],[data-cv],[data-pre]",
    );
    if (!t) return;
    last = t;
    if (t.dataset.tog) {
      const j = db.jobs[+t.dataset.tog];
      j[3] = j[3] === "Active" ? "Closed" : "Active";
      renderAll();
      toast(j[0] + " is now " + j[3].toLowerCase() + ".");
    }
    if (t.dataset.st) {
      const [i, s] = t.dataset.st.split(":"),
        a = db.apps[+i];
      a[4] = s;
      renderAll();
      toast(a[0] + " marked as " + s.toLowerCase() + ".");
    }
    if (t.dataset.cv) {
      const a = db.apps[+t.dataset.cv],
        u = URL.createObjectURL(
          new Blob(
            [
              "Sample resume for " +
                a[0] +
                "\n" +
                a[1] +
                "\nDemonstration file only.",
            ],
            { type: "text/plain" },
          ),
        ),
        l = document.createElement("a");
      l.href = u;
      l.download = a[0].replace(/ /g, "_") + "_Resume.txt";
      document.body.append(l);
      l.click();
      l.remove();
      setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(u), 500);
      toast("Resume of " + a[0] + " downloaded (sample file).");
    }
    if (t.dataset.iv) {
      ivI = +t.dataset.iv;
      $("#diW").textContent = "Applicant: " + db.apps[ivI][0];
      clearForm($("#fI"));
      $("#iD").min = today();
      $("#dI").showModal();
    }
    if (t.dataset.pre) {
      $("#hT").value = t.dataset.pre;
      $("#hT").scrollIntoView({
        block: "center",
        behavior: reduced ? "auto" : "smooth",
      });
      $("#hT").focus({ preventScroll: true });
    }
  });
  $("#newJob").onclick = (e) => {
    last = e.currentTarget;
    clearForm($("#fJ"));
    $("#dJ").showModal();
  };
  ["dJ", "dI"].forEach((id) => {
    const d = $("#" + id);
    d.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
      if (e.target === d || e.target.closest("[data-x]")) {
        d.close();
        last && last.focus();
      }
    });
  });
  $("#fJ").addEventListener("submit", (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!vForm(e.target)) return;
    const t = $("#vtitle").value.trim();
    db.jobs.unshift([
      t,
      $("#vloc").value.trim(),
      +$("#vslots").value,
      "Pending",
      $("#vpay").value.trim(),
      $("#vcat").value,
    ]);
    db.apps.forEach((a) => a[3]++);
    $("#dJ").close();
    renderAll();
    mtab("p");
    toast('"' + t + '" submitted. It will go live after PESO approval.');
    last && last.focus();
  });
  $("#fI").addEventListener("submit", (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!vForm(e.target)) return;
    const a = db.apps[ivI],
      d = new Date($("#iD").value + "T" + $("#iT").value),
      w =
        d.toLocaleDateString("en-PH", {
          month: "short",
          day: "numeric",
        }) +
        ", " +
        d.toLocaleTimeString("en-PH", {
          hour: "numeric",
          minute: "2-digit",
        });
    a[4] = "Interview Â· " + w;
    $("#dI").close();
    renderAll();
    toast("Interview with " + a[0] + " set for " + w + ".");
    last && last.focus();
  });
  $("#fH").addEventListener("submit", (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!vForm(e.target)) return;
    const r = rid("REQ-");
    db.reqs.unshift([r, $("#hT").value, $("#hN").value]);
    clearForm(e.target);
    renderAll();
    toast("Request sent. Your reference is " + r + ".");
  });

  /* ---- restore session on load; keep tabs in sync ---- */
  addEventListener("storage", (e) => {
    if (e.key !== SKEY) return;
    let s = null;
    try {
      s = e.newValue && JSON.parse(e.newValue);
    } catch (_) {}
    if (!s && user) {
      stopTimers();
      user = null;
      show("landing");
    } else if (s && !user) enter(s, true);
  });
  try {
    const s = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(SKEY) || "null");
    if (
      s &&
      (s.role === "jobseeker" || s.role === "employer") &&
      typeof s.name === "string" &&
      s.name
    )
      enter(s, true);
  } catch (e) {}
})();


===== /opt/lampp/htdocs/peso-portal/js/auth.js =====
(function () {
  "use strict";
  /* ---------- registration wizard ---------- */
  const form = document.getElementById("joinForm");
  if (form) {
    const roleInput = document.getElementById("roleInput");
    const tabs = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("[data-role]"));
    const panels = Array.from(form.querySelectorAll("[data-step]"));
    const roleOnly = Array.from(form.querySelectorAll("[data-role-only]"));
    const steps = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("#wizSteps li"));
    const bar = document.getElementById("wizBar");
    const head = document.getElementById("wizHead");
    const alertBox = document.getElementById("formAlert");
    let step = 1;

    const controls = (el) =>
      el.matches("input,select,textarea,button")
        ? [el]
        : Array.from(el.querySelectorAll("input,select,textarea,button"));

    function applyRole() {
      const role = roleInput.value;
      roleOnly.forEach((el) => {
        const mine = el.dataset.roleOnly === role;
        el.hidden = !mine;
        const panel = el.closest("[data-step]");
        const panelOn = panel && !panel.hidden;
        controls(el).forEach((c) => {
          c.disabled = !(mine && panelOn);
        });
      });
      if (head) head.hidden = role === "employer";
    }

    function showStep(n, dir) {
      step = n;
      panels.forEach((p) => {
        const on = Number(p.dataset.step) === n;
        p.hidden = !on;
        controls(p).forEach((c) => {
          c.disabled = !on;
        });
        p.classList.remove("next", "prev");
        if (on && dir) p.classList.add(dir);
      });
      steps.forEach((li, i) => {
        li.classList.toggle("on", i === n - 1);
        li.classList.toggle("done", i < n - 1);
      });
      if (bar) bar.style.width = (n / 3) * 100 + "%";
      applyRole();
      const h = panels.find((p) => !p.hidden)?.querySelector("h3");
      if (h) {
        h.setAttribute("tabindex", "-1");
        h.focus();
      }
    }

    function setRole(role) {
      roleInput.value = role;
      tabs.forEach((t) =>
        t.setAttribute("aria-selected", String(t.dataset.role === role)),
      );
      showStep(1);
      if (alertBox) alertBox.classList.remove("show");
    }

    function fieldOf(el) {
      return el.closest(".field");
    }

    function validate(panel) {
      let ok = true,
        first = null;
      panel.querySelectorAll("input,select,textarea").forEach((el) => {
        if (el.disabled) return;
        const f = fieldOf(el);
        let bad = !el.checkValidity();
        if (el.id === "confirm_password") {
          bad =
            el.value !== document.getElementById("password").value || !el.value;
        }
        if (f) f.classList.toggle("bad", bad);
        if (bad) {
          ok = false;
          first = first || el;
        }
      });
      if (first) first.focus();
      return ok;
    }

    form.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
      const next = e.target.closest("[data-next]");
      const back = e.target.closest("[data-back]");
      if (next) {
        const panel = panels.find((p) => !p.hidden);
        if (validate(panel)) {
          if (alertBox) alertBox.classList.remove("show");
          showStep(step + 1, "next");
        } else if (alertBox) {
          alertBox.textContent = "Please correct the highlighted fields.";
          alertBox.classList.add("show");
        }
      }
      if (back) showStep(step - 1, "prev");
    });

    form.addEventListener("input", (e) => {
      const f = fieldOf(e.target);
      if (f && f.classList.contains("bad") && e.target.checkValidity())
        f.classList.remove("bad");
    });

    /* =========================================================
		FIXED SUBMIT HANDLER
		Ensures fields are ENABLED before submission so the browser
		actually sends their values to PHP.
		========================================================= */
    form.addEventListener("submit", (e) => {
      let badStep = null;
      // 1. Validate all relevant steps
      panels.forEach((p) => {
        const isJobseeker = roleInput.value === "jobseeker";
        const relevant = isJobseeker || p.dataset.step === "1";
        if (!relevant) return;

        const wasHidden = p.hidden;
        if (wasHidden) p.hidden = false;
        controls(p).forEach((c) => {
          const roleOnly = c.closest("[data-role-only]");
          if (!roleOnly || roleOnly.dataset.roleOnly === roleInput.value) {
            c.disabled = false;
          }
        });

        if (!validate(p) && !badStep) {
          badStep = Number(p.dataset.step);
        }
      });

      // 2. If validation failed, stop submission and go to the bad step
      if (badStep) {
        e.preventDefault();
        showStep(badStep);
        if (alertBox) {
          alertBox.textContent =
            "Please correct the highlighted fields before submitting.";
          alertBox.classList.add("show");
        }
        return;
      }

      // 3. CRITICAL FIX: Ensure all relevant fields are ENABLED for submission.
      panels.forEach((p) => {
        const isJobseeker = roleInput.value === "jobseeker";
        const relevant = isJobseeker || p.dataset.step === "1";
        if (!relevant) return;
        controls(p).forEach((c) => {
          const roleOnly = c.closest("[data-role-only]");
          if (!roleOnly || roleOnly.dataset.roleOnly === roleInput.value) {
            c.disabled = false;
          }
        });
      });

      // 4. Disable fields from the OTHER role so they aren't submitted
      roleOnly.forEach((el) => {
        if (el.dataset.roleOnly !== roleInput.value) {
          controls(el).forEach((c) => (c.disabled = true));
        }
      });

      // 5. Prevent double submission
      const btn = form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]:not([disabled])');
      if (btn) {
        btn.disabled = true;
        btn.textContent = "Creating accountâ€¦";
      }
    });

    tabs.forEach((t) =>
      t.addEventListener("click", () => setRole(t.dataset.role)),
    );

    document
      .querySelectorAll("[data-role-link]")
      .forEach((a) =>
        a.addEventListener("click", () => setRole(a.dataset.roleLink)),
      );

    const preset = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get("role");
    setRole(preset === "employer" ? "employer" : "jobseeker");
  }

  /* ---------- password reveal ---------- */
  // REMOVED: This logic was duplicated in script.js.
  // Having both caused them to fire sequentially and cancel each other out.
  // script.js already handles all ".pw button" clicks globally.

  /* ---------- file name preview ---------- */
  document.querySelectorAll('input[type="file"]').forEach((input) => {
    input.addEventListener("change", () => {
      const label = document.querySelector(
        '[data-file-label="' + input.id + '"]',
      );
      if (label && input.files[0])
        label.textContent = "Selected: " + input.files[0].name;
    });
  });

  /* ---------- copy tracking ID ---------- */
  document.querySelectorAll("[data-copy]").forEach((btn) => {
    btn.addEventListener("click", async () => {
      try {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(btn.dataset.copy);
        btn.textContent = "Copied";
        setTimeout(() => {
          btn.textContent = "Copy ID";
        }, 1800);
      } catch (_) {
        /* clipboard unavailable */
      }
    });
  });

  /* ---------- generic dialog close ---------- */
  document
    .querySelectorAll("[data-close]")
    .forEach((btn) =>
      btn.addEventListener("click", () => btn.closest("dialog")?.close()),
    );
})();


===== /opt/lampp/htdocs/peso-portal/db.php =====
<?php
declare(strict_types=1);

if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}

$dsn = 'mysql:host=127.0.0.1;dbname=peso_portal;charset=utf8mb4';

try {
    $pdo = new PDO($dsn, 'root', '', [
        PDO::ATTR_ERRMODE            => PDO::ERRMODE_EXCEPTION,
        PDO::ATTR_DEFAULT_FETCH_MODE => PDO::FETCH_ASSOC,
        PDO::ATTR_EMULATE_PREPARES   => false,
    ]);
} catch (PDOException $e) {
    http_response_code(500);
    exit('Database connection failed. Start MySQL in XAMPP and import schema.sql.');
}

===== /opt/lampp/htdocs/peso-portal/.git/hooks/fsmonitor-watchman.sample =====
#!/usr/bin/perl

use strict;
use warnings;
use IPC::Open2;

# An example hook script to integrate Watchman
# (https://facebook.github.io/watchman/) with git to speed up detecting
# new and modified files.
#
# The hook is passed a version (currently 2) and last update token
# formatted as a string and outputs to stdout a new update token and
# all files that have been modified since the update token. Paths must
# be relative to the root of the working tree and separated by a single NUL.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "query-watchman" and set
# 'git config core.fsmonitor .git/hooks/query-watchman'
#
my ($version, $last_update_token) = @ARGV;

# Uncomment for debugging
# print STDERR "$0 $version $last_update_token\n";

# Check the hook interface version
if ($version ne 2) {
	die "Unsupported query-fsmonitor hook version '$version'.\n" .
	    "Falling back to scanning...\n";
}

my $git_work_tree = get_working_dir();

my $retry = 1;

my $json_pkg;
eval {
	require JSON::XS;
	$json_pkg = "JSON::XS";
	1;
} or do {
	require JSON::PP;
	$json_pkg = "JSON::PP";
};

launch_watchman();

sub launch_watchman {
	my $o = watchman_query();
	if (is_work_tree_watched($o)) {
		output_result($o->{clock}, @{$o->{files}});
	}
}

sub output_result {
	my ($clockid, @files) = @_;

	# Uncomment for debugging watchman output
	# open (my $fh, ">", ".git/watchman-output.out");
	# binmode $fh, ":utf8";
	# print $fh "$clockid\n@files\n";
	# close $fh;

	binmode STDOUT, ":utf8";
	print $clockid;
	print "\0";
	local $, = "\0";
	print @files;
}

sub watchman_clock {
	my $response = qx/watchman clock "$git_work_tree"/;
	die "Failed to get clock id on '$git_work_tree'.\n" .
		"Falling back to scanning...\n" if $? != 0;

	return $json_pkg->new->utf8->decode($response);
}

sub watchman_query {
	my $pid = open2(\*CHLD_OUT, \*CHLD_IN, 'watchman -j --no-pretty')
	or die "open2() failed: $!\n" .
	"Falling back to scanning...\n";

	# In the query expression below we're asking for names of files that
	# changed since $last_update_token but not from the .git folder.
	#
	# To accomplish this, we're using the "since" generator to use the
	# recency index to select candidate nodes and "fields" to limit the
	# output to file names only. Then we're using the "expression" term to
	# further constrain the results.
	my $last_update_line = "";
	if (substr($last_update_token, 0, 1) eq "c") {
		$last_update_token = "\"$last_update_token\"";
		$last_update_line = qq[\n"since": $last_update_token,];
	}
	my $query = <<"	END";
		["query", "$git_work_tree", {$last_update_line
			"fields": ["name"],
			"expression": ["not", ["dirname", ".git"]]
		}]
	END

	# Uncomment for debugging the watchman query
	# open (my $fh, ">", ".git/watchman-query.json");
	# print $fh $query;
	# close $fh;

	print CHLD_IN $query;
	close CHLD_IN;
	my $response = do {local $/; <CHLD_OUT>};

	# Uncomment for debugging the watch response
	# open ($fh, ">", ".git/watchman-response.json");
	# print $fh $response;
	# close $fh;

	die "Watchman: command returned no output.\n" .
	"Falling back to scanning...\n" if $response eq "";
	die "Watchman: command returned invalid output: $response\n" .
	"Falling back to scanning...\n" unless $response =~ /^\{/;

	return $json_pkg->new->utf8->decode($response);
}

sub is_work_tree_watched {
	my ($output) = @_;
	my $error = $output->{error};
	if ($retry > 0 and $error and $error =~ m/unable to resolve root .* directory (.*) is not watched/) {
		$retry--;
		my $response = qx/watchman watch "$git_work_tree"/;
		die "Failed to make watchman watch '$git_work_tree'.\n" .
		    "Falling back to scanning...\n" if $? != 0;
		$output = $json_pkg->new->utf8->decode($response);
		$error = $output->{error};
		die "Watchman: $error.\n" .
		"Falling back to scanning...\n" if $error;

		# Uncomment for debugging watchman output
		# open (my $fh, ">", ".git/watchman-output.out");
		# close $fh;

		# Watchman will always return all files on the first query so
		# return the fast "everything is dirty" flag to git and do the
		# Watchman query just to get it over with now so we won't pay
		# the cost in git to look up each individual file.
		my $o = watchman_clock();
		$error = $output->{error};

		die "Watchman: $error.\n" .
		"Falling back to scanning...\n" if $error;

		output_result($o->{clock}, ("/"));
		$last_update_token = $o->{clock};

		eval { launch_watchman() };
		return 0;
	}

	die "Watchman: $error.\n" .
	"Falling back to scanning...\n" if $error;

	return 1;
}

sub get_working_dir {
	my $working_dir;
	if ($^O =~ 'msys' || $^O =~ 'cygwin') {
		$working_dir = Win32::GetCwd();
		$working_dir =~ tr/\\/\//;
	} else {
		require Cwd;
		$working_dir = Cwd::cwd();
	}

	return $working_dir;
}


===== /opt/lampp/htdocs/peso-portal/.git/hooks/sendemail-validate.sample =====
#!/bin/sh

# An example hook script to validate a patch (and/or patch series) before
# sending it via email.
#
# The hook should exit with non-zero status after issuing an appropriate
# message if it wants to prevent the email(s) from being sent.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "sendemail-validate".
#
# By default, it will only check that the patch(es) can be applied on top of
# the default upstream branch without conflicts in a secondary worktree. After
# validation (successful or not) of the last patch of a series, the worktree
# will be deleted.
#
# The following config variables can be set to change the default remote and
# remote ref that are used to apply the patches against:
#
#   sendemail.validateRemote (default: origin)
#   sendemail.validateRemoteRef (default: HEAD)
#
# Replace the TODO placeholders with appropriate checks according to your
# needs.

validate_cover_letter () {
	file="$1"
	# TODO: Replace with appropriate checks (e.g. spell checking).
	true
}

validate_patch () {
	file="$1"
	# Ensure that the patch applies without conflicts.
	git am -3 "$file" || return
	# TODO: Replace with appropriate checks for this patch
	# (e.g. checkpatch.pl).
	true
}

validate_series () {
	# TODO: Replace with appropriate checks for the whole series
	# (e.g. quick build, coding style checks, etc.).
	true
}

# main -------------------------------------------------------------------------

if test "$GIT_SENDEMAIL_FILE_COUNTER" = 1
then
	remote=$(git config --default origin --get sendemail.validateRemote) &&
	ref=$(git config --default HEAD --get sendemail.validateRemoteRef) &&
	worktree=$(mktemp --tmpdir -d sendemail-validate.XXXXXXX) &&
	git worktree add -fd --checkout "$worktree" "refs/remotes/$remote/$ref" &&
	git config --replace-all sendemail.validateWorktree "$worktree"
else
	worktree=$(git config --get sendemail.validateWorktree)
fi || {
	echo "sendemail-validate: error: failed to prepare worktree" >&2
	exit 1
}

unset GIT_DIR GIT_WORK_TREE
cd "$worktree" &&

if grep -q "^diff --git " "$1"
then
	validate_patch "$1"
else
	validate_cover_letter "$1"
fi &&

if test "$GIT_SENDEMAIL_FILE_COUNTER" = "$GIT_SENDEMAIL_FILE_TOTAL"
then
	git config --unset-all sendemail.validateWorktree &&
	trap 'git worktree remove -ff "$worktree"' EXIT &&
	validate_series
fi


===== /opt/lampp/htdocs/peso-portal/.git/hooks/update.sample =====
#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to block unannotated tags from entering.
# Called by "git receive-pack" with arguments: refname sha1-old sha1-new
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "update".
#
# Config
# ------
# hooks.allowunannotated
#   This boolean sets whether unannotated tags will be allowed into the
#   repository.  By default they won't be.
# hooks.allowdeletetag
#   This boolean sets whether deleting tags will be allowed in the
#   repository.  By default they won't be.
# hooks.allowmodifytag
#   This boolean sets whether a tag may be modified after creation. By default
#   it won't be.
# hooks.allowdeletebranch
#   This boolean sets whether deleting branches will be allowed in the
#   repository.  By default they won't be.
# hooks.denycreatebranch
#   This boolean sets whether remotely creating branches will be denied
#   in the repository.  By default this is allowed.
#

# --- Command line
refname="$1"
oldrev="$2"
newrev="$3"

# --- Safety check
if [ -z "$GIT_DIR" ]; then
	echo "Don't run this script from the command line." >&2
	echo " (if you want, you could supply GIT_DIR then run" >&2
	echo "  $0 <ref> <oldrev> <newrev>)" >&2
	exit 1
fi

if [ -z "$refname" -o -z "$oldrev" -o -z "$newrev" ]; then
	echo "usage: $0 <ref> <oldrev> <newrev>" >&2
	exit 1
fi

# --- Config
allowunannotated=$(git config --type=bool hooks.allowunannotated)
allowdeletebranch=$(git config --type=bool hooks.allowdeletebranch)
denycreatebranch=$(git config --type=bool hooks.denycreatebranch)
allowdeletetag=$(git config --type=bool hooks.allowdeletetag)
allowmodifytag=$(git config --type=bool hooks.allowmodifytag)

# check for no description
projectdesc=$(sed -e '1q' "$GIT_DIR/description")
case "$projectdesc" in
"Unnamed repository"* | "")
	echo "*** Project description file hasn't been set" >&2
	exit 1
	;;
esac

# --- Check types
# if $newrev is 0000...0000, it's a commit to delete a ref.
zero=$(git hash-object --stdin </dev/null | tr '[0-9a-f]' '0')
if [ "$newrev" = "$zero" ]; then
	newrev_type=delete
else
	newrev_type=$(git cat-file -t $newrev)
fi

case "$refname","$newrev_type" in
	refs/tags/*,commit)
		# un-annotated tag
		short_refname=${refname##refs/tags/}
		if [ "$allowunannotated" != "true" ]; then
			echo "*** The un-annotated tag, $short_refname, is not allowed in this repository" >&2
			echo "*** Use 'git tag [ -a | -s ]' for tags you want to propagate." >&2
			exit 1
		fi
		;;
	refs/tags/*,delete)
		# delete tag
		if [ "$allowdeletetag" != "true" ]; then
			echo "*** Deleting a tag is not allowed in this repository" >&2
			exit 1
		fi
		;;
	refs/tags/*,tag)
		# annotated tag
		if [ "$allowmodifytag" != "true" ] && git rev-parse $refname > /dev/null 2>&1
		then
			echo "*** Tag '$refname' already exists." >&2
			echo "*** Modifying a tag is not allowed in this repository." >&2
			exit 1
		fi
		;;
	refs/heads/*,commit)
		# branch
		if [ "$oldrev" = "$zero" -a "$denycreatebranch" = "true" ]; then
			echo "*** Creating a branch is not allowed in this repository" >&2
			exit 1
		fi
		;;
	refs/heads/*,delete)
		# delete branch
		if [ "$allowdeletebranch" != "true" ]; then
			echo "*** Deleting a branch is not allowed in this repository" >&2
			exit 1
		fi
		;;
	refs/remotes/*,commit)
		# tracking branch
		;;
	refs/remotes/*,delete)
		# delete tracking branch
		if [ "$allowdeletebranch" != "true" ]; then
			echo "*** Deleting a tracking branch is not allowed in this repository" >&2
			exit 1
		fi
		;;
	*)
		# Anything else (is there anything else?)
		echo "*** Update hook: unknown type of update to ref $refname of type $newrev_type" >&2
		exit 1
		;;
esac

# --- Finished
exit 0

===== /opt/lampp/htdocs/peso-portal/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample =====
#!/bin/sh
#
# Copyright (c) 2006, 2008 Junio C Hamano
#
# The "pre-rebase" hook is run just before "git rebase" starts doing
# its job, and can prevent the command from running by exiting with
# non-zero status.
#
# The hook is called with the following parameters:
#
# $1 -- the upstream the series was forked from.
# $2 -- the branch being rebased (or empty when rebasing the current branch).
#
# This sample shows how to prevent topic branches that are already
# merged to 'next' branch from getting rebased, because allowing it
# would result in rebasing already published history.

publish=next
basebranch="$1"
if test "$#" = 2
then
	topic="refs/heads/$2"
else
	topic=`git symbolic-ref HEAD` ||
	exit 0 ;# we do not interrupt rebasing detached HEAD
fi

case "$topic" in
refs/heads/??/*)
	;;
*)
	exit 0 ;# we do not interrupt others.
	;;
esac

# Now we are dealing with a topic branch being rebased
# on top of master.  Is it OK to rebase it?

# Does the topic really exist?
git show-ref -q "$topic" || {
	echo >&2 "No such branch $topic"
	exit 1
}

# Is topic fully merged to master?
not_in_master=`git rev-list --pretty=oneline ^master "$topic"`
if test -z "$not_in_master"
then
	echo >&2 "$topic is fully merged to master; better remove it."
	exit 1 ;# we could allow it, but there is no point.
fi

# Is topic ever merged to next?  If so you should not be rebasing it.
only_next_1=`git rev-list ^master "^$topic" ${publish} | sort`
only_next_2=`git rev-list ^master           ${publish} | sort`
if test "$only_next_1" = "$only_next_2"
then
	not_in_topic=`git rev-list "^$topic" master`
	if test -z "$not_in_topic"
	then
		echo >&2 "$topic is already up to date with master"
		exit 1 ;# we could allow it, but there is no point.
	else
		exit 0
	fi
else
	not_in_next=`git rev-list --pretty=oneline ^${publish} "$topic"`
	/usr/bin/perl -e '
		my $topic = $ARGV[0];
		my $msg = "* $topic has commits already merged to public branch:\n";
		my (%not_in_next) = map {
			/^([0-9a-f]+) /;
			($1 => 1);
		} split(/\n/, $ARGV[1]);
		for my $elem (map {
				/^([0-9a-f]+) (.*)$/;
				[$1 => $2];
			} split(/\n/, $ARGV[2])) {
			if (!exists $not_in_next{$elem->[0]}) {
				if ($msg) {
					print STDERR $msg;
					undef $msg;
				}
				print STDERR " $elem->[1]\n";
			}
		}
	' "$topic" "$not_in_next" "$not_in_master"
	exit 1
fi

<<\DOC_END

This sample hook safeguards topic branches that have been
published from being rewound.

The workflow assumed here is:

 * Once a topic branch forks from "master", "master" is never
   merged into it again (either directly or indirectly).

 * Once a topic branch is fully cooked and merged into "master",
   it is deleted.  If you need to build on top of it to correct
   earlier mistakes, a new topic branch is created by forking at
   the tip of the "master".  This is not strictly necessary, but
   it makes it easier to keep your history simple.

 * Whenever you need to test or publish your changes to topic
   branches, merge them into "next" branch.

The script, being an example, hardcodes the publish branch name
to be "next", but it is trivial to make it configurable via
$GIT_DIR/config mechanism.

With this workflow, you would want to know:

(1) ... if a topic branch has ever been merged to "next".  Young
    topic branches can have stupid mistakes you would rather
    clean up before publishing, and things that have not been
    merged into other branches can be easily rebased without
    affecting other people.  But once it is published, you would
    not want to rewind it.

(2) ... if a topic branch has been fully merged to "master".
    Then you can delete it.  More importantly, you should not
    build on top of it -- other people may already want to
    change things related to the topic as patches against your
    "master", so if you need further changes, it is better to
    fork the topic (perhaps with the same name) afresh from the
    tip of "master".

Let's look at this example:

		   o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o "next"
		  /       /           /           /
		 /   a---a---b A     /           /
		/   /               /           /
	       /   /   c---c---c---c B         /
	      /   /   /             \         /
	     /   /   /   b---b C     \       /
	    /   /   /   /             \     /
    ---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o "master"


A, B and C are topic branches.

 * A has one fix since it was merged up to "next".

 * B has finished.  It has been fully merged up to "master" and "next",
   and is ready to be deleted.

 * C has not merged to "next" at all.

We would want to allow C to be rebased, refuse A, and encourage
B to be deleted.

To compute (1):

	git rev-list ^master ^topic next
	git rev-list ^master        next

	if these match, topic has not merged in next at all.

To compute (2):

	git rev-list master..topic

	if this is empty, it is fully merged to "master".

DOC_END


===== /opt/lampp/htdocs/peso-portal/.git/hooks/pre-receive.sample =====
#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to make use of push options.
# The example simply echoes all push options that start with 'echoback='
# and rejects all pushes when the "reject" push option is used.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "pre-receive".

if test -n "$GIT_PUSH_OPTION_COUNT"
then
	i=0
	while test "$i" -lt "$GIT_PUSH_OPTION_COUNT"
	do
		eval "value=\$GIT_PUSH_OPTION_$i"
		case "$value" in
		echoback=*)
			echo "echo from the pre-receive-hook: ${value#*=}" >&2
			;;
		reject)
			exit 1
		esac
		i=$((i + 1))
	done
fi


===== /opt/lampp/htdocs/peso-portal/.git/hooks/commit-msg.sample =====
#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to check the commit log message.
# Called by "git commit" with one argument, the name of the file
# that has the commit message.  The hook should exit with non-zero
# status after issuing an appropriate message if it wants to stop the
# commit.  The hook is allowed to edit the commit message file.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "commit-msg".

# Uncomment the below to add a Signed-off-by line to the message.
# Doing this in a hook is a bad idea in general, but the prepare-commit-msg
# hook is more suited to it.
#
# SOB=$(git var GIT_AUTHOR_IDENT | sed -n 's/^\(.*>\).*$/Signed-off-by: \1/p')
# grep -qs "^$SOB" "$1" || echo "$SOB" >> "$1"

# This example catches duplicate Signed-off-by lines.

test "" = "$(grep '^Signed-off-by: ' "$1" |
	 sort | uniq -c | sed -e '/^[ 	]*1[ 	]/d')" || {
	echo >&2 Duplicate Signed-off-by lines.
	exit 1
}

===== /opt/lampp/htdocs/peso-portal/.git/hooks/push-to-checkout.sample =====
#!/bin/sh

# An example hook script to update a checked-out tree on a git push.
#
# This hook is invoked by git-receive-pack(1) when it reacts to git
# push and updates reference(s) in its repository, and when the push
# tries to update the branch that is currently checked out and the
# receive.denyCurrentBranch configuration variable is set to
# updateInstead.
#
# By default, such a push is refused if the working tree and the index
# of the remote repository has any difference from the currently
# checked out commit; when both the working tree and the index match
# the current commit, they are updated to match the newly pushed tip
# of the branch. This hook is to be used to override the default
# behaviour; however the code below reimplements the default behaviour
# as a starting point for convenient modification.
#
# The hook receives the commit with which the tip of the current
# branch is going to be updated:
commit=$1

# It can exit with a non-zero status to refuse the push (when it does
# so, it must not modify the index or the working tree).
die () {
	echo >&2 "$*"
	exit 1
}

# Or it can make any necessary changes to the working tree and to the
# index to bring them to the desired state when the tip of the current
# branch is updated to the new commit, and exit with a zero status.
#
# For example, the hook can simply run git read-tree -u -m HEAD "$1"
# in order to emulate git fetch that is run in the reverse direction
# with git push, as the two-tree form of git read-tree -u -m is
# essentially the same as git switch or git checkout that switches
# branches while keeping the local changes in the working tree that do
# not interfere with the difference between the branches.

# The below is a more-or-less exact translation to shell of the C code
# for the default behaviour for git's push-to-checkout hook defined in
# the push_to_deploy() function in builtin/receive-pack.c.
#
# Note that the hook will be executed from the repository directory,
# not from the working tree, so if you want to perform operations on
# the working tree, you will have to adapt your code accordingly, e.g.
# by adding "cd .." or using relative paths.

if ! git update-index -q --ignore-submodules --refresh
then
	die "Up-to-date check failed"
fi

if ! git diff-files --quiet --ignore-submodules --
then
	die "Working directory has unstaged changes"
fi

# This is a rough translation of:
#
#   head_has_history() ? "HEAD" : EMPTY_TREE_SHA1_HEX
if git cat-file -e HEAD 2>/dev/null
then
	head=HEAD
else
	head=$(git hash-object -t tree --stdin </dev/null)
fi

if ! git diff-index --quiet --cached --ignore-submodules $head --
then
	die "Working directory has staged changes"
fi

if ! git read-tree -u -m "$commit"
then
	die "Could not update working tree to new HEAD"
fi


===== /opt/lampp/htdocs/peso-portal/.git/hooks/pre-commit.sample =====
#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to verify what is about to be committed.
# Called by "git commit" with no arguments.  The hook should
# exit with non-zero status after issuing an appropriate message if
# it wants to stop the commit.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "pre-commit".

if git rev-parse --verify HEAD >/dev/null 2>&1
then
	against=HEAD
else
	# Initial commit: diff against an empty tree object
	against=$(git hash-object -t tree /dev/null)
fi

# If you want to allow non-ASCII filenames set this variable to true.
allownonascii=$(git config --type=bool hooks.allownonascii)

# Redirect output to stderr.
exec 1>&2

# Cross platform projects tend to avoid non-ASCII filenames; prevent
# them from being added to the repository. We exploit the fact that the
# printable range starts at the space character and ends with tilde.
if [ "$allownonascii" != "true" ] &&
	# Note that the use of brackets around a tr range is ok here, (it's
	# even required, for portability to Solaris 10's /usr/bin/tr), since
	# the square bracket bytes happen to fall in the designated range.
	test $(git diff-index --cached --name-only --diff-filter=A -z $against |
	  LC_ALL=C tr -d '[ -~]\0' | wc -c) != 0
then
	cat <<\EOF
Error: Attempt to add a non-ASCII file name.

This can cause problems if you want to work with people on other platforms.

To be portable it is advisable to rename the file.

If you know what you are doing you can disable this check using:

  git config hooks.allownonascii true
EOF
	exit 1
fi

# If there are whitespace errors, print the offending file names and fail.
exec git diff-index --check --cached $against --


===== /opt/lampp/htdocs/peso-portal/.git/hooks/pre-push.sample =====
#!/bin/sh

# An example hook script to verify what is about to be pushed.  Called by "git
# push" after it has checked the remote status, but before anything has been
# pushed.  If this script exits with a non-zero status nothing will be pushed.
#
# This hook is called with the following parameters:
#
# $1 -- Name of the remote to which the push is being done
# $2 -- URL to which the push is being done
#
# If pushing without using a named remote those arguments will be equal.
#
# Information about the commits which are being pushed is supplied as lines to
# the standard input in the form:
#
#   <local ref> <local oid> <remote ref> <remote oid>
#
# This sample shows how to prevent push of commits where the log message starts
# with "WIP" (work in progress).

remote="$1"
url="$2"

zero=$(git hash-object --stdin </dev/null | tr '[0-9a-f]' '0')

while read local_ref local_oid remote_ref remote_oid
do
	if test "$local_oid" = "$zero"
	then
		# Handle delete
		:
	else
		if test "$remote_oid" = "$zero"
		then
			# New branch, examine all commits
			range="$local_oid"
		else
			# Update to existing branch, examine new commits
			range="$remote_oid..$local_oid"
		fi

		# Check for WIP commit
		commit=$(git rev-list -n 1 --grep '^WIP' "$range")
		if test -n "$commit"
		then
			echo >&2 "Found WIP commit in $local_ref, not pushing"
			exit 1
		fi
	fi
done

exit 0


===== /opt/lampp/htdocs/peso-portal/.git/hooks/prepare-commit-msg.sample =====
#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to prepare the commit log message.
# Called by "git commit" with the name of the file that has the
# commit message, followed by the description of the commit
# message's source.  The hook's purpose is to edit the commit
# message file.  If the hook fails with a non-zero status,
# the commit is aborted.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "prepare-commit-msg".

# This hook includes three examples. The first one removes the
# "# Please enter the commit message..." help message.
#
# The second includes the output of "git diff --name-status -r"
# into the message, just before the "git status" output.  It is
# commented because it doesn't cope with --amend or with squashed
# commits.
#
# The third example adds a Signed-off-by line to the message, that can
# still be edited.  This is rarely a good idea.

COMMIT_MSG_FILE=$1
COMMIT_SOURCE=$2
SHA1=$3

/usr/bin/perl -i.bak -ne 'print unless(m/^. Please enter the commit message/..m/^#$/)' "$COMMIT_MSG_FILE"

#  *) ;;

# git ls-files --others --exclude-from=.git/info/exclude
# Lines that start with '#' are comments.

===== /opt/lampp/htdocs/peso-portal/appbar.php =====
<?php
// Security check: Redirect to home if not logged in
if (empty($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
    header('Location: peso-portal.html');
    exit;
}
$isEm = ($_SESSION['role'] ?? '') === 'employer';
$name = htmlspecialchars($_SESSION['name'] ?? 'User', ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8');
?>
<header class="appbar">
  <div class="container">
    <a href="dashboard.php" class="brand">
      <span class="mark">PESO</span>
      <span>
        <strong>PESO Online</strong>
        <small>Member area</small>
      </span>
    </a>
    <div class="tools">
      <span class="pill"><?= $isEm ? 'Employer' : 'Jobseeker' ?></span>
      <button class="btn btn-glass bell" type="button" aria-label="Notifications">
        ğŸ””<span class="badge">0</span>
      </button>
      <div class="me">
        <div class="av"><?= strtoupper(substr($name, 0, 1)) ?></div>
        <div>
          <small>Signed in as</small>
          <b><?= $name ?></b>
        </div>
      </div>
      <a href="logout.php" class="btn btn-glass">Log out</a>
    </div>
  </div>
</header>
<main id="main">

===== /opt/lampp/htdocs/peso-portal/register_success.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/db.php';
if (empty($_SESSION['just_registered'])) { header('Location: peso-portal.html#join'); exit; }
unset($_SESSION['just_registered']);

// Set the page title for the browser tab
$pageTitle = 'Account created';

// Include the top HTML and the Logged-in Navigation
require_once __DIR__ . '/header.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/appbar.php'; 
?>

<section class="block">
  <div class="container">
    <div class="shell">
      <div class="confirm">
        <div class="ticket">
          <div>
            <p class="tag"><?= $isEm ? 'Employer account' : 'Application tracking ID' ?></p>
            <p class="id"><?= htmlspecialchars($_SESSION['tracking_id'], ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8') ?></p>
            <p>Thank you, <?= htmlspecialchars($_SESSION['name'], ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8') ?>. We will contact you at your registered email. Keep this ID to follow your application.</p>

===== /opt/lampp/htdocs/peso-portal/register.php =====
<?php
declare(strict_types=1);
require_once __DIR__ . '/db.php';

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] !== 'POST') {
    header('Location: index.html#join');
    exit;
}

function val(string $key): string {
    return trim((string)($_POST[$key] ?? ''));
}

function fail(array $errors): void {
    http_response_code(422);
    $items = '';
    foreach ($errors as $e) {
        $items .= '<li>' . htmlspecialchars($e, ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8') . '</li>';
    }
    echo '<!doctype html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8">'
       . '<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">'
       . '<title>Registration problem â€” PESO</title>'
       . '<link rel="stylesheet" href="styles.css"></head><body>'
       . '<section class="block"><div class="container"><div class="shell"><div class="wpanel">'
       . '<h3>We could not create your account</h3>'
       . '<p class="help">Please go back and fix the following:</p>'
       . '<div class="alert show"><ul>' . $items . '</ul></div>'
       . '<div class="acts"><a class="btn btn-p" href="index.html#join">Back to registration</a>'
       . '<button class="btn btn-line" type="button" onclick="history.back()">Keep my entries</button>'
       . '</div></div></div></div></section></body></html>';
    exit;
}

function upload_doc(string $field, string $folder, bool $required, array &$errors): array {
    if (!isset($_FILES[$field]) || $_FILES[$field]['error'] === UPLOAD_ERR_NO_FILE) {
        if ($required) $errors[] = 'Please attach your resume (PDF, DOC or DOCX).';
        return [null, null];
    }
    $f = $_FILES[$field];
    if ($f['error'] !== UPLOAD_ERR_OK) { $errors[] = 'The upload failed. Please try again.'; return [null, null]; }
    if ($f['size'] > 5 * 1024 * 1024)  { $errors[] = 'The file must be 5 MB or smaller.';   return [null, null]; }

    $allowed = [
        'application/pdf' => 'pdf',
        'application/msword' => 'doc',
        'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document' => 'docx',
    ];
    $mime = (new finfo(FILEINFO_MIME_TYPE))->file($f['tmp_name']);
    if (!isset($allowed[$mime])) { $errors[] = 'Only PDF, DOC or DOCX files are accepted.'; return [null, null]; }

    $dir = __DIR__ . '/uploads/' . $folder;
    if (!is_dir($dir)) mkdir($dir, 0775, true);

    $stored = bin2hex(random_bytes(16)) . '.' . $allowed[$mime];
    if (!move_uploaded_file($f['tmp_name'], $dir . '/' . $stored)) {
        $errors[] = 'The file could not be saved on the server.';
        return [null, null];
    }
    return [$stored, substr(basename($f['name']), 0, 255)];
}

/* ---------- shared fields ---------- */
$role    = val('role');
$first   = val('first_name');
$last    = val('last_name');
$email   = mb_strtolower(val('email'));
$mobile  = val('mobile');
$pass    = (string)($_POST['password'] ?? '');
$confirm = (string)($_POST['confirm_password'] ?? '');
$consent = val('consent') === '1';
$errors  = [];

if (!in_array($role, ['jobseeker', 'employer'], true)) $errors[] = 'Please choose an account type.';
if ($first === '')  $errors[] = 'First name is required.';
if ($last === '')   $errors[] = 'Last name is required.';
if (!filter_var($email, FILTER_VALIDATE_EMAIL)) $errors[] = 'A valid email address is required.';
if (!preg_match('/^[0-9+\s()\-]{7,20}$/', $mobile)) $errors[] = 'A valid mobile number is required.';
if (strlen($pass) < 8)   $errors[] = 'Password must be at least 8 characters.';
if ($pass !== $confirm)  $errors[] = 'Passwords do not match.';
if (!$consent)           $errors[] = 'You must agree to the data privacy statement.';

/* ---------- role-specific fields ---------- */
if ($role === 'jobseeker') {
    $birthdate = val('birthdate');
    $d = DateTime::createFromFormat('Y-m-d', $birthdate);
    if (!$d || $d->format('Y-m-d') !== $birthdate) $errors[] = 'A valid date of birth is required.';

    $sex      = val('sex');
    $barangay = val('barangay');
    $city     = val('city');
    $educ     = val('highest_education');
    $course   = val('course');
    $skills   = val('skills');
    $years    = max(0, min(60, (int)val('experience_years')));
    $jobType  = val('preferred_job_type');
    $location = val('preferred_location');
    $salary   = val('expected_salary') === '' ? null : (float)val('expected_salary');
    $avail    = val('availability');

    if ($sex === '')       $errors[] = 'Please select your sex.';
    if ($barangay === '')  $errors[] = 'Barangay is required.';
    if ($city === '')      $errors[] = 'City or municipality is required.';
    if ($educ === '')      $errors[] = 'Highest education is required.';
    if ($skills === '')    $errors[] = 'Please list at least one skill.';
    if ($jobType === '')   $errors[] = 'Work arrangement is required.';
    if ($location === '')  $errors[] = 'Preferred location is required.';
    if ($avail === '')     $errors[] = 'Availability is required.';

    [$resumeFile, $resumeOrig] = upload_doc('resume', 'resumes', true, $errors);
} else {
    $company  = val('company_name');
    $industry = val('industry');
    $position = val('position_title');
    $size     = val('company_size');
    $permitNo = val('business_permit_no');
    $address  = val('company_address');
    $website  = val('website');
    $hiring   = max(0, min(9999, (int)val('hiring_count')));

    if ($company === '')  $errors[] = 'Company name is required.';
    if ($industry === '') $errors[] = 'Industry is required.';
    if ($position === '') $errors[] = 'Your position is required.';
    if ($permitNo === '') $errors[] = 'Business permit / DTI / SEC number is required.';
    if ($address === '')  $errors[] = 'Company address is required.';
    if ($website !== '' && !filter_var($website, FILTER_VALIDATE_URL)) $errors[] = 'Website must be a valid URL.';

    [$permitFile, $permitOrig] = upload_doc('business_permit', 'permits', false, $errors);
}

/* ---------- duplicate email ---------- */
if (!$errors) {
    $check = $pdo->prepare('SELECT id FROM users WHERE email = ? LIMIT 1');
    $check->execute([$email]);
    if ($check->fetch()) $errors[] = 'That email address is already registered. Please log in instead.';
}

if ($errors) fail($errors);

/* ---------- save ---------- */
$trackingId = 'PESO-' . date('Y') . '-' . strtoupper(bin2hex(random_bytes(3)));
$hash       = password_hash($pass, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
$status     = $role === 'employer' ? 'pending' : 'active';

try {
    $pdo->beginTransaction();

    $pdo->prepare(
        'INSERT INTO users (tracking_id, role, email, password_hash, first_name, last_name, mobile, status)
         VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)'
    )->execute([$trackingId, $role, $email, $hash, $first, $last, $mobile, $status]);

    $userId = (int)$pdo->lastInsertId();

    if ($role === 'jobseeker') {
        $pdo->prepare(
            'INSERT INTO jobseeker_profiles
             (user_id, birthdate, sex, barangay, city, highest_education, course, skills,
              experience_years, resume_filename, resume_original, preferred_job_type,
              preferred_location, expected_salary, availability, consent)
             VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,1)'
        )->execute([
            $userId, $birthdate, $sex, $barangay, $city, $educ, $course, $skills,
            $years, $resumeFile, $resumeOrig, $jobType, $location, $salary, $avail
        ]);
    } else {
        $pdo->prepare(
            'INSERT INTO employer_profiles
             (user_id, company_name, industry, position_title, company_size, business_permit_no,
              company_address, website, hiring_count, permit_filename, permit_original, consent)
             VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,1)'
        )->execute([
            $userId, $company, $industry, $position, $size, $permitNo,
            $address, $website, $hiring, $permitFile, $permitOrig
        ]);
    }

    $pdo->commit();
} catch (Throwable $e) {
    if ($pdo->inTransaction()) $pdo->rollBack();
    error_log('PESO registration: ' . $e->getMessage());
    fail(['Something went wrong while saving your account. Please try again.']);
}

/* ---------- log in + show ticket ---------- */
session_regenerate_id(true);
$_SESSION['user_id']     = $userId;
$_SESSION['role']        = $role;
$_SESSION['name']        = $first . ' ' . $last;
$_SESSION['tracking_id'] = $trackingId;
$_SESSION['status']      = $status;
$_SESSION['just_registered'] = true;

header('Location: register_success.php');
exit;

===== /opt/lampp/htdocs/peso-portal/login.php =====
<?php // login.php
declare(strict_types=1);
require_once __DIR__ . '/db.php';
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] !== 'POST') { header('Location: peso-portal.html'); exit; }

$email = mb_strtolower(trim((string)($_POST['email'] ?? '')));
$pass  = (string)($_POST['password'] ?? '');

$stmt = $pdo->prepare('SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ? LIMIT 1');
$stmt->execute([$email]);
$u = $stmt->fetch();

// Verify credentials only (role is no longer checked against a form input)
if (!$u || !password_verify($pass, $u['password_hash'])) {
    http_response_code(401);
    echo '<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><link rel="stylesheet" href="css/styles.css">'
        . '<section class="block"><div class="container"><div class="shell"><div class="wpanel">'
        . '<h3>Log in failed</h3><p class="help">Wrong email or password.</p>'
        . '<div class="acts"><a class="btn btn-p" href="peso-portal.html">Back</a></div>'
        . '</div></div></div></section>';
    exit;
}

// Automatically use the role stored in the database
$role = $u['role'];

session_regenerate_id(true);
$_SESSION['user_id']     = (int)$u['id'];
$_SESSION['role']        = $role;
$_SESSION['name']        = $u['first_name'] . ' ' . $u['last_name'];
$_SESSION['tracking_id'] = $u['tracking_id'];
$_SESSION['status']      = $u['status'];

header('Location: dashboard.php');
exit;
